##  NIT Trichy CAT Cutoff 2026 
Students can take admission in MBA programs at NIT Trichy based on [CAT](https://collegedunia.com/exams/cat) score. As per NIT Trichy CAT Cutoff 2025, the overall cutoff for MBA programs in Round 1 is 80 across all categories. 
MBA General, is the top choice for students appearing in CAT. 
In 2025, MBA General closing percentile for General Category is 80 in Round 1. 
You can check MBA General, category-wise closing percentile for CAT below: 
###  NIT Trichy MBA General Cutoff 2025   
|  Category   |  Closing percentile   |  
| --- | --- |  
|  General   |  80   |  
##  NIT Trichy CAT Cutoff Trends

  * GATE

The **GATE 2025 cutoff for NIT Trichy** shows a score range from **777 to 351** for admission to **M.Tech and M.Arch** programs in the General category. For **M.Tech Computer Science and Engineering** , the **Round 1 cutoff score is 777** and the **last round cutoff is 739**. **M.Tech Data Analytics** recorded a **Round 1 cutoff of 749** and a **last round cutoff of 702**. Other competitive courses, including **VLSI System Design, Power Electronics, and Communication Systems** , have cutoffs in the **702 to 534** range.
Less sought-after branches such as **Industrial Metallurgy, Non Destructive Testing, and Environmental Engineering** show cutoffs between **397 and 351**. For **OBC** candidates, the admission cutoff range is **731 for Computer Science and Engineering** to **311 for Energy Efficient And Sustainable Architecture**. In the **SC** category, the score range is **595 for Computer Science and Engineering** to **168 for Material Science and Engineering**. For **ST** candidates, the cutoff is **473 for Computer Science and Engineering** , extending to **164 for Energy Efficient And Sustainable Architecture**.
Under the **EWS** category, the cutoff range is from **760 for Computer Science and Engineering** to **315 for Chemical Engineering**.

## Can I get admission into NIT Trichy with 97 percentile in JEE Main?
Shivendra Soni
97 percentile in JEE Main for NIT Trichy — here's the honest answer:
NIT Trichy is one of the top 5 NITs. 97 percentile = roughly rank 30,000-35,000.
**For home state Tamil Nadu (HS quota):** 97 percentile gives decent chances for Civil, Mechanical, Chemical, Electrical branches. CSE and ECE at NIT Trichy HS typically close around 99+ percentile, so those won't open at 97.
**For outside Tamil Nadu (OS quota):** 97 percentile is not enough for NIT Trichy in any branch through OS quota. NIT Trichy OS cutoffs are extremely competitive — even Civil/Mech OS closes around 98.5-99+ percentile. At 97 percentile outside TN, NIT Trichy is very unlikely.
what to do at 97 percentile OS:
  * NIT Trichy OS is out, but consider: NIT Calicut HS (if Kerala), MNNIT Allahabad, NIT Warangal, MNIT Jaipur — all have better chances at 97 percentile OS
  * Apply to all JoSAA rounds — cutoffs drop round by round
  * If Tamil Nadu HS: NIT Trichy Civil/Mech/Chemical are realistic


check JoSAA 2024 closing ranks for exact figures. 97 percentile is a solid score for mid-tier NITs OS or top NITs HS in non-CS branches.

## How much mark in JEE Mains is sufficient for NIT Trichy?
Pranav Kumar
JEE Main marks required for NIT Trichy — this is one of the most Googled cutoff questions, and the answer is highly nuanced by branch, category, and state.
**JEE Main marks/percentile for NIT Trichy (2024 JoSAA data):**
  * **CSE (most competitive):** For General/OS (Outside State): Typically requires **99+ percentile** (absolute score ~280+/300). Home state Tamil Nadu quota: ~97-98 percentile.
  * **ECE:** General/OS: ~98-99 percentile. Home state: ~96-97 percentile.
  * **Mechanical / Civil:** General/OS: ~94-96 percentile. Home state: ~92-94 percentile.
  * **Less competitive branches (Production, Metallurgy):** General/OS: ~90-93 percentile.


**Marks vs. Percentile context:**
  * 99 percentile ? 230-250 marks out of 300 (depends on paper difficulty and number of test-takers).
  * These cutoffs shift 2-5 percentile points each year based on overall difficulty.


**Category consideration:**
  * OBC: Approximately 3-4 percentile lower than General for same branch.
  * SC: ~10-12 percentile lower.
  * ST: ~15-20 percentile lower.


Use **JoSAA historic cutoff data (josaa.nic.in)** for the most accurate branch/round/category breakdown from last 3 years. NIT Trichy CSE is genuinely one of the hardest NIT branches to get into — only IIT branches with similar difficulty are reference.

## How much should I score in JEE mains paper 2 to get a seat in NIT Trichy?
Shankar PandeyB.Tech Production and Industrial Engineering, National Institute of Technology, Tiruchirappalli (2020)
To get admission to the undergraduate architecture program at the National Institute of Technology (NIT) Trichy through JEE Main Paper 2, you need to score a rank within the cutoff range set by the institute.
The cutoff rank for admission to NIT Trichy through JEE Main Paper 2 varies each year and depends on various factors, such as the number of applicants, the difficulty level of the exam, and the availability of seats in the architecture program. However, based on the previous year's cutoff trends, you need to score a JEE Main Paper 2 rank within the top 4,000-5,000 to have a good chance of getting admission to the architecture program at NIT Trichy.
It's important to note that meeting the cutoff rank is only one of the eligibility criteria for admission to NIT Trichy. The institute considers other factors such as the candidate's academic performance, the number of applicants for a program, and the reservation policies, among others, for final admission decisions.
